TL;DR: 当评估者改变而候选响应固定时，LLM-as-judge评分仍会变动。本文将此视为测量效度问题，通过对比Qwen3参数升级和MiniMax API升级两种路径发现：法官升级不可互换，仅Qwen3从1.7B到4B有稳健增益；强法官减少但未消除位置和冗长偏差；相关性错误下重复陪审团效果有限；结构化辩论可大幅改变决策但无解析器和回退日志无法归因于 deliberation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge评分会因评估者替换而产生不确定性，这种评估者替换歧义本质上是测量效度问题，需要系统性的实证研究来理解其影响模式和边界条件。

Method: 在四个判断数据集上，对比Qwen3密集法官从1.7B到32B参数规模升级，以及MiniMax M2-M2.7跨发布版本API升级这两种实际可用的升级路径，分析位置偏差、冗长偏差、相关性误差和结构化辩论的影响。

Result: 法官升级不可互换；仅Qwen3 1.7B到4B有稳健相邻增益，MiniMax相邻版本无此效果；强法官减少但未消除偏差；相关性错误下重复陪审团增益有限；结构化辩论可大幅改变决策但缺乏审计追踪。

Conclusion: LLM-as-judge报告应包含数据集切片、偏差探测、误差依赖性估计和协议审计追踪。

Abstract: An LLM-as-judge score can move even when the candidate responses stay fixed, simply because the evaluator has changed. We treat this evaluator-replacement ambiguity as a measurement-validity problem. Across four judgment datasets, we compare two upgrade paths available in practice: scaling Qwen3 dense judges from 1.7B to 32B parameters and moving across MiniMax M2-M2.7 released APIs. The main pattern is that judge upgrades are not interchangeable: only Qwen3 1.7B to 4B gives a robust adjacent gain, while MiniMax adjacent releases do not. Stronger judges reduce but do not remove position and verbosity bias. Repeated-sample juries add little when errors are correlated. Structured debate can move decisions substantially, but without parser and fallback logs those shifts cannot be attributed to deliberation. We argue that LLM-as-judge reports should include dataset slices, bias probes, error-dependence estimates, and protocol audit trails.

</details>


### [38] [It Takes a MAESTRO To Prune Bad Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08601)
*Palaash Goel,Ayush Maheshwari,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MAESTRO是一种针对稀疏激活MoE语言模型的结构化剪枝框架，通过将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，利用其平稳分布捕捉跨层依赖关系，生成全局感知的重要性启发式，在50%压缩率下平均性能保持比SOTA方法高10.61%，且跨任务方差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化剪枝方法主要针对稠密Transformer设计，使用基于局部启发式的方法评估专家重要性，忽略了MoE路由的相互依赖特性，导致剪枝后的模型在推理时仍需要将所有专家加载到内存中，造成部署瓶颈。

Method: 将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，计算其平稳分布来编码跨层依赖关系，从而生成全局感知的专家重要性度量，以此指导结构化剪枝。

Result: 在包含安全、偏见、伦理等五个不同领域的评估中，在50%压缩率下，MAESTRO在平均性能保持上比最先进基线方法高出10.61%，且跨任务方差显著更低。

Conclusion: 全局的、与路由一致的剪枝方法能够生成在不同异构任务上更一致泛化的模型，证明了考虑MoE路由相互依赖性的重要性。

Abstract: Sparsely-activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models achieve remarkable inference efficiency by activating only a small fraction of parameters per token, yet their full expert banks reside in memory at all times, creating a prohibitive deployment bottleneck. Existing structured pruning methods, largely designed for dense transformers, assess expert importance using locally derived heuristics that are blind to the interdependent nature of MoE routing. We introduce MAESTRO (Markov-chain Approximated Expert Sparsification via Transition-based ROuting), a structured pruning framework designed for MoE architectures that models autoregressive expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains whose stationary distributions encode cross-layer dependencies, yielding a globally aware importance heuristic. Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.

</details>


### [39] [DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08642)
*Saw S. Lin,Jyh-Shing Roger Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DominoTree是一种无需训练的投机解码方法，结合Domino的条件非因子化校正与最佳优先树扩展，在Qwen3-4B上实现最高6.6x加速比，平均接受长度达10.7 tokens，并通过GPU原生CUDA图构建器保持高效吞吐，优于DFlash、DDTree、CaDDTree和Domino等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码方法存在局限：块扩散草稿器（如DFlash）只建模逐位置边缘分布，而最佳优先树方法（如DDTree）基于这些边缘分布扩展候选树，无法表示路径依赖结构。Domino虽引入GRU因果校正使分布路径依赖，但其因子化形式限制了树的表达能力，且未见与树结构方法的结合。

Method: 提出DominoTree，将Domino的条件非因子化校正应用于最佳优先草稿树的每个根到节点路径上。为实用化，将每个节点的校正限制在候选top-M范围内。树构建采用GPU原生CUDA图实现，与Python参考实现比特一致，保证接受率不变且构建成本低。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B的8个基准上：最高6.6x加速比；所有温度下平均接受长度最高达10.7 tokens；吞吐量在每个温度下均优于Domino解码器（整体9-10%，Alpaca上+22%），优于DDTree/CaDDTree；在Qwen3-8B上保持最高接受长度，T=0时吞吐量+24%优于DDTree。

Conclusion: DominoTree通过将路径依赖校正与最佳优先树搜索结合，在不增加训练成本的情况下显著提升投机解码的接受长度和吞吐量，在多个模型和温度设置下一致优于现有方法。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce
  a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The
  released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized
  formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction
  along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree
  reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every
  temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so
  acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released
  Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3-
  8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature
  that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.

</details>


### [40] [UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08646)
*Xinlong Zhao,Dongsheng Liu,Hengyu Zhao,Zixuan Fu,Zheng Wang,Jie Cai,Jie Zhou,Qiang Ma,Xuanhe Zhou,Xu Han,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大规模预训练数据面临的质量、效率和可靠性挑战，提出UltraX函数调用精炼框架，通过引入插入操作扩展编辑函数空间，结合可编程监督生成与稳定推理执行，在更少训练token下实现更优的数据利用效率与精炼可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据接近物理极限，Scaling Laws带来的增益逐渐减弱，提升LLM愈发依赖更高质量的数据利用。现有精炼方法（基于规则的受限于固定启发式，基于LLM的无法满足大规模处理的高效与可靠需求）存在明显局限。

Method: 提出UltraX框架：(1) 在删除和修改之外引入插入操作，完成编辑函数空间；(2) 构建可靠的程序监督生成管道，包括数据集自适应提示优化、Line Alignment Mapping和Dynamic Context Replacement将文本对转换为结构化程序监督；(3) 通过低置信度样本过滤和按操作组合的比例控制采样提升监督质量与训练分布稳定性；(4) 推理执行阶段采用滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合和系统性后处理，提升大规模执行的稳定性与可靠性。

Result: UltraX在所有语料上取得最高平均性能，并在更少训练token下匹配或超越基线方法，展现出更强的数据效率和精炼可靠性。

Conclusion: UltraX通过函数调用范式实现了细粒度的实例级编辑，在质量、效率和可靠性之间取得了更好的平衡，为大规模预训练数据精炼提供了一种有效的新方案。

Abstract: As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.

</details>


### [41] [WebSwarm: Recursive Multi-Agent Orchestration for Deep-and-Wide Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08662)
*Xiaoshuai Song,Liancheng Zhang,Kangzhi Zhao,Yutao Zhu,Zhongyuan Wang,Guanting Dong,Jinghan Yang,Han Li,Kun Gai,Ji-Rong Wen,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WebSwarm提出了一种渐进式递归委托框架，通过动态实例化搜索节点、任务分解与递归扩展，以及跨节点经验复用，在深度与广度交织的复杂搜索任务上显著优于单智能体和现有多智能体基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ReAct风格单智能体受限于单条长轨迹和有限上下文，难以同时处理搜索深度和覆盖广度；现有的多智能体系统虽然通过并行执行和聚合提升了搜索覆盖，但在递归深度、协作适应性和基于证据的扩展方面仍存在明显局限。

Method: WebSwarm是一个渐进式递归委托框架，包含三个关键机制：(1) 动态实例化智能体搜索节点，每个节点耦合局部目标与搜索模式；(2) 节点可自行求解或委托子节点，求解后将证据和结果向上返回，支持父节点进一步扩展、修订或聚合；(3) 先探测网络信息组织方式以指导节点扩展，并在同质兄弟节点间复用过程级经验。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch和GISA四个基准上，WebSwarm在深度任务、广度任务以及深度-广度交织任务上均一致优于单智能体和多智能体基线方法。

Conclusion: WebSwarm通过渐进式递归委托框架有效解决了复杂搜索任务中的深度与覆盖问题，消融实验、任务难度分析、网络工具效率分析和模型泛化分析进一步验证了其有效性，并为多智能体搜索系统提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.

</details>

*Manuel Pita*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑国家语言模型在测量理论构念（如道德基础中的权威）时的有效性：一致性（agreement）仅反映可靠性（reliability），不能保证效度（validity），模型可能通过表面相关性捷径而非遵循理论规则得出正确编码。

Method: 提出“恢复差距”（recovery gap）测试方法：将整体提示分解为编码本中的原子条款，再按理论的显式规则重新组合，比较分解前后性能损失；同时测试校准后的英语工具向AMALIA-9B和欧洲葡萄牙语的迁移性。

Result: AMALIA-9B与人类编码者的F1差距在6分以内（对比8-13倍规模的开源模型），但分解后仅恢复约一半的整体性能；误差分析表明模型依赖表面相关性（如权威人物附近的道德愤怒）；校准的英语工具无法成功迁移；开源多语言LLM在相同葡萄牙语语料和指令下能缩小恢复差距。

Conclusion: AMALIA-9B可用于筛选和预编码，但尚不能独立可靠地测量该理论构念；主权LLM基准测试应增加对一致性证据路径的检验，而不仅限于人类一致性的比较。

Abstract: A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.

</details>


### [44] [UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08768)
*Zhekai Chen,Chengqi Duan,Kaiyue Sun,Bohao Li,Yuqing Wang,Manyuan Zhang,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniClawBench 是首个基于能力驱动的基准测试，用于在动态真实环境中评估主动智能体。它围绕五种基础模型能力设计400个双语真实任务，采用实时Docker容器评估和闭环多轮反馈策略，系统性地解耦了基础模型能力与框架设计对智能体性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试依赖沙盒环境和单轮评估范式，且其基于场景的任务分类将多种模型能力混杂在同一任务类别中，难以定位智能体失败的根因，因此亟需一种能够解耦不同能力、在真实动态环境中评估主动智能体的新基准。

Method: 构建五个基础模型能力维度（技能使用、探索、长上下文推理、多模态理解、跨平台协调），设计400个双语真实任务；采用实时Docker容器进行细粒度逐步骤完成检查；设计包含执行智能体、隐藏监督智能体和用户智能体的闭环评估策略，模拟多轮人类反馈且不泄露评分标准；在多种智能体框架下评估多个模型以解耦能力与框架影响。

Result: 通过跨模型与框架的综合比较，揭示了基础模型能力与智能体框架设计如何共同影响真实环境中的性能表现。

Conclusion: UniClawBench 能够有效解耦基础模型能力与框架设计的影响，为主动智能体在真实环境中的评估提供了更全面、更细粒度的基准，代码已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用满二叉树对解进行显式展开，将系统解耦为双线性子系统序列；基于此定义有限时域和无限时域的可达性/可观测性格拉姆矩阵，通过非线性矩阵方程刻画存在性条件；利用格拉姆矩阵的核消除得到精确降阶模型，并发展平衡截断方法。

Abstract: We investigate quadratic bilinear systems by developing novel tree-based representations of their solutions. The proposed framework decomposes the solution into a sequence of coupled bilinear subsystems whose components admit explicit expansions indexed by full binary trees. These representations yield sufficient conditions for the existence of global solutions and lead to new output bounds in terms of reachability Gramians. Motivated by these estimates, we introduce time-limited and infinite-horizon reachability and observability Gramians, establish sufficient conditions for their existence, and characterize them through nonlinear matrix equations. The associated Gramians are employed to identify dominant state-spaces and to derive exact reduced-order models obtained by removing Gramian kernels. Building on these results, we develop a balanced truncation method for quadratic bilinear systems and prove an error bound for the reduced-order approximation. The proposed framework provides a unified connection between tree-based solution representations, nonlinear Gramian theory, and balanced truncation for quadratic bilinear systems, closing several theoretical gaps in the analysis of Gramian-based model reduction for this class of systems.

</details>

Abstract: We study approximation and structural simplification of optimal control policies for controlled regime-switching diffusion processes for discounted, ergodic, finite-horizon, and exit-time criteria. We first establish continuity of the cost functionals over classes of Markov and stationary Markov policies by exploiting elliptic and parabolic regularity of the corresponding Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman and Poisson equations. Using density results of policies with finite-action, piecewise-constant, and Lipschitz continuous, we show that each control problem admits $\varepsilon$-optimal policies within these structured subclasses. We then construct an Euler--Maruyama approximation of the controlled regime-switching diffusion under piecewise-constant controls. We prove strong convergence of the controlled state process and establish convergence of the associated finite-horizon value functions with rate $O(h^{γ/2})$. Building on this discretization, we develop a finite-state approximation of the induced discrete-time Markov chain via state-space quantization. We show that the value functions of the finite models converge uniformly on compact sets to the value function of the original problem, and that optimal policies of the approximating models are asymptotically optimal.
  These results provide a systematic framework for approximating regime-switching diffusion control problems and justify the use of structured policies and finite-state models for numerical implementation.

</details>

Abstract: Among the many types of acceleration techniques designed to improve the performance of Benders decomposition, unified cut generation schemes have recently attracted a keen interest. Unified cuts aim for a better balance between the generation of optimality and feasibility cuts, while also providing a way to compare the strengths of different feasibility cuts. Our goal is to assess the experimental performance of a broad selection of unified and distinct Benders cuts in the context of the multi-commodity fixed-charge network design problem (MCFNDP).
  We express under a common mathematical structure and notation the construction of each unified or distinct Benders cut considered. We also explain how the generic formulations of the Benders cuts can be specialized to conform to the specifications of the MCFNDP. In addition, we suggest bespoke methods for comparing the performance of several solution methods when the benchmark is made up of heterogeneous problem instances. We report the results of a systematic empirical analysis comparing the performances of 50 Benders methods involving unified or distinct cuts in applications to a common testing bench made up of standardized MCFNDP instances. The analysis identifies a small number of leading Benders methods, namely those featuring the static Brandenberg-Stursberg cuts and the Hosseini-Turner l1-deepest cuts. In addition, we also report results obtained by using both Gurobi and CPLEX as the supporting solver to the SMS++ computation library.

### [48] [Local Linear Convergence of the Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient Method for Semidefinite Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08035)
*Xin Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究原始-对偶混合梯度(PDHG)方法在半定规划(SDP)中的局部收敛性，证明在严格互补性或原始-对偶非退化性条件下PDHG最终(R-)线性收敛，并给出一个两者均失效时仅能次线性收敛的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管原始-对偶一阶方法被广泛用于大规模半定规划(SDP)，但全局收敛理论无法解释它们为何能计算高精度解，因此需要从局部收敛角度深入分析PDHG方法的收敛行为。

Method: 将PDHG视为KKT包含的预处理近端点方法，结合其下降不等式与局部误差界进行分析。在严格互补性条件下，误差界源于正半定锥的局部谱几何；在原始-对偶非退化性条件下，误差界源于KKT映射的强正则性。

Result: PDHG在满足严格互补性或原始-对偶非退化性时最终(R-)线性收敛；给出了一个两种条件均不满足的简单SDP实例，PDHG仅能次线性收敛；数值实验验证了理论并识别了PDHG难以达到高精度的困难SDP实例。

Conclusion: 与线性规划中PDHG即使在退化情形也能局部线性收敛不同，SDP中当严格互补性和原始-对偶非退化性同时失效时，PDHG的收敛速度会降为次线性。

Abstract: Primal-dual first-order methods are widely used for large-scale semidefinite programming (SDP), but their ability to compute highly accurate solutions is not well explained by global convergence theory alone. We study the local convergence of the primal-dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) method applied to a standard primal--dual SDP pair. Abstract: In this paper, we investigate a class of so-called sub-infinite horizon stochastic linear-quadratic optimal control problems, in which the initial time $t$ is arbitrarily taken from $[0,\infty)$ and the running cost is defined over $[t,t+T]$ for a given $T>0$. Abstract: This article concerns interacting particle systems with singular kernels, driven either by degenerate deterministic controls or by degenerate decomposable noise. Numerical experiments illustrate the predicted rate, the dimensional scaling of the constant, and its asymptotic sharpness.

</details>


### [59] [Finding Simple Proofs for First-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08753)
*Daniel Berg Thomsen,Manu Upadhyaya,Baptiste Goujaud,Aymeric Dieuleveut,Adrien Taylor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将优化器生成的密集的拉格朗日对偶证书通过稀疏优化和统计学习工具后处理，蒸馏出结构简洁、可解释、可复用的证明结构，并在梯度下降、近邻方法和快速梯度方法上验证了该方法能自动剪枝冗余不等式、发现结构化证明模式，甚至恢复Lyapunov函数作为中间引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动认证系统能证明结果成立，但返回的是密集的证书而非可解释、可复用的证明结构。数学进步不仅需要真值认证，更需要透明、可检验、可复用的证明结构。

Method: 将寻找简化证明结构建模为对偶证书上的第二阶段优化问题；采用穷举稀疏化、加权ℓ₁型启发式、半定规划等后处理程序；以活动假设和残差结构等特征度量复杂度。

Result: 在梯度下降、近邻方法和快速梯度方法上，该方法能自动剪枝冗余不等式、揭示结构化证明模式，并在近邻设置中恢复Lyapunov函数作为中间引理，生成简洁流线型的证明。

Conclusion: 该工作流作为最终证明的预处理步骤，将密集的机器生成证书蒸馏为紧凑的证明结构，降低了人类理解、复现和形式化过程中需要管理的复杂度。

Abstract: Progress in mathematics often requires more than a certificate of truth: it requires proof structures that are transparent, checkable, and reusable. Automated systems can increasingly certify that a result is true; what they typically return, however, is a dense certificate rather than an interpretable, reusable proof structure.
  Recent work on performance estimation problems has shown that performance bounds and complexity analyses of first-order optimization methods can be discovered by searching over a structured space of Lagrangian dual certificates. We cast the search for simpler proof structures as a second-stage optimization problem over these certificates. Starting from dual certificates, we develop post-processing procedures using tools from sparse optimization and statistical learning. We measure complexity through features such as active hypotheses and residual structure, and introduce methods based on exhaustive sparsification, weighted $\ell_1$-type heuristics, and semidefinite programming (SDP) formulations for discovering simple proofs and intermediate lemmas.
  Examples on gradient descent, proximal methods, and fast-gradient methods show that these procedures can autonomously prune redundant inequalities, reveal structured proof patterns, and, in the proximal setting, recover Lyapunov functions as intermediate lemmas that lead to simple, streamlined proofs. By distilling dense machine-generated certificates into compact proof structures, this workflow acts as a pre-processing step for the final proof, reducing the complexity that must be managed during human interpretation, reuse, and formalization.

</details>

### [60] [Context Graphs for Proactive Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07721)
*Avinash Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Context Graph的主动式智能代理系统，通过持续监控企业实体状态变化并计算主动性分数，在用户提问前推送相关信息。在合同管理、工程响应和销售管道三个场景中，Precision@5达0.83，假阳性率0.11，平均呈现时间从47分钟降至30秒以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG和代理系统本质上是反应式的——需要等待用户查询才能行动。企业生产力提升需要主动式系统，能够在工人提问之前就推送相关、可操作的信息。

Method: 构建Context Graph（实时关系数据结构，建模企业实体、关系及状态变迁）；设计Delta Detection Engine（持续监控状态变化）；Proactivity Scorer（按紧迫性、相关性、角色匹配度排序候选信息）；Surfacing Layer（LLM驱动，推送带解释的排名通知）。统一Proactivity Score函数，并用NetworkX和Claude API实现端到端系统。

Result: 在合同生命周期管理、工程事件响应和销售管道卫生三个企业案例中，Precision@5为0.83，假阳性率0.11，平均呈现时间从反应式基线的47分钟降至30秒以内。

Conclusion: 基于上下文图的主动式代理系统能有效提升企业生产力，大幅降低信息呈现延迟，验证了从反应式向主动式范式转变的可行性和价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and agentic frameworks have advanced enterprise AI considerably, yet agents remain fundamentally reactive: they wait for a human query before acting. This paper argues that genuine enterprise productivity gains require proactive agents: systems that surface relevant, actionable information to workers before they ask. We propose the Context Graph, a live relational data structure that models enterprise entities, their relationships, and state transitions over time. Built on this graph, we define a Delta Detection Engine that continuously monitors state changes, a Proactivity Scorer that ranks candidate insights by urgency, relevance, and persona-fit, and a Surfacing Layer powered by an LLM that delivers ranked notifications with grounded explanations. We formalize each component, derive a unified Proactivity Score function, and provide a complete end-to-end Python implementation using NetworkX and the Anthropic Claude API. Evaluation across three generic enterprise case studies (contract lifecycle management, engineering incident response, and sales pipeline hygiene) demonstrates that context-graph-driven proactivity achieves Precision@5 of 0.83, a false positive rate of 0.11, and reduces mean time to surface from 47 minutes (reactive baseline) to under 30 second.

</details>

*Joshua R. Waite,Dana Golden,Brett Indelicato,Kevin Camp,Mojdeh Saadati,Shannon Regan,Patrick Schnable,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian,Carlos Messina,Suzanne Thornsbury,Soumik Sarkar*

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个AI工具，整合经济模型（GTAP）与生物物理模型（APSIM），通过自然语言查询和响应，帮助决策者和市场参与者评估农业供应链中跨学科冲击的影响。

Method: 将经济模型（GTAP）与生物物理模型（APSIM）进行集成，构建AI驱动的分析工具，支持用户通过自然语言进行查询并获取跨学科影响评估结果。

Result: 成功开发了一个AI工具，能够整合经济和生物物理模型，通过自然语言交互方式评估供应链冲击的跨学科影响。

Conclusion: 该AI工具为政策制定者和市场参与者提供了一种便捷的方式，通过自然语言交互评估农业供应链冲击的跨学科影响。

Abstract: Agricultural supply chains are vulnerable to disruptions through linked biophysical and economic systems. We develop an AI-powered tool that integrates economic models (GTAP) with biophysical models (APSIM) to analyze supply chain shocks, enabling policymakers and market participants to assess cross-disciplinary impacts through queries and responses written in natural language.

</details>


### [62] [Adversarial Social Epistemology for Assemblies of Humans and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07760)
*Mihnea C. Moldoveanu,Joel A. C. Baum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性社会认识论（ASE），用于分析密集交互的传播环境中，代理人如何利用证言链、制度认证和隐性信任来扭曲、省略或伪造信息，并提供了审计和补救信任破坏的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念（如认知气泡、回音室、错误信息扩散）不足以解释在支架式公开传播中，代理人如何利用通常使断言值得信任的承诺和权利来破坏信任。

Method: 采用对抗性社会认识论框架，结合推理主义语义学，通过认知网络对断言进行解释，并设计审计和补救信任破坏的机制。

Result: 提供了分析支架式通信中信任破坏所需的语言，描述了颠覆信任的具体机制，并建立了用于审计和补救的可操作框架。

Conclusion: 对抗性社会认识论能够有效解释和应对密集传播环境中利用系统信任机制进行信息操纵的现象，弥补了现有理论的不足。

Abstract: We outline an adversarial social epistemology (ASE) for densely interactive communicative landscapes in which public assertions are scaffolded by chains of testimony, inference, institutional certification, and tacit trust. In such landscapes, agents have incentives and affordances to distort, color, omit, fabricate, or strategically under-specify information for private, reputational, rhetorical, or material gains. We argue that these phenomena are not adequately captured by familiar descriptions of epistemic bubbles, echo chambers, or misinformation diffusion. What requires explanation is how communicative agents exploit the commitments and entitlements that normally make scaffolded assertions trustworthy. We provide language that delivers the requisite analysis, outline mechanisms that subvert trust in scaffolded public communications, and outline machinery for auditing and redressing trust breaches arising from subverting the auditability of inferential chains, drawing on epistemic networks, enriched with an inferentialist semantics for interpreting assertions.

</details>


### [63] [Aligning Clinical Needs and AI Capabilities: A Survey on LLMs for Medical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07761)
*Qi Peng,Jiatong Li,Sirui Huang,Yiyang Jiang,Kaisong Gong,Ronger Ding,Shijie Ye,Changmeng Zheng,Yi Cai,Xiaobo Yang,Jin Huang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述论文探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在医疗领域的推理应用，提出了连接临床实践与计算方法的双重视角，建立了基于Miller金字塔的五级能力框架，并将演绎、归纳、溯因推理模式与医疗任务对应，通过18个前沿模型的基准测试揭示了不同模型在诊断、决策支持等任务中的表现差异，最后讨论了数据限制、幻觉和接地问题等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs在医疗领域日益重要，现有研究缺乏系统性地连接临床推理需求与计算方法的框架，需要建立统一的评估体系来理解不同模型在医疗推理任务中的能力差异。

Method: 采用双重视角方法：临床侧基于Miller金字塔构建五级能力体系（知识回忆→动态病例管理）；计算侧将演绎、归纳、溯因三种推理模式映射到医疗目标和任务。同时构建跨五级医疗推理能力的基准数据集，对18个最先进模型进行系统评估。

Result: 医疗专科模型在诊断中心任务上表现优异，而通用模型在决策支持和对话领域领先。揭示了不同推理模式与医疗任务之间的对应关系，以及当前模型在各类医疗推理能力上的差异化表现。

Conclusion: 虽然医疗LLMs取得显著进展，但仍面临数据限制、幻觉和接地问题等挑战，需要朝着更安全、更可靠、更适配临床工作流的方向发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as important tools in healthcare, showing growing potential for clinical reasoning and patient care. This survey examines recent progress in medical LLMs, focusing on reasoning applications and requirements. We present a dual-view approach that connects clinical practice with computational methods. On the clinical side, we establish a five-level competency scheme following Miller's Pyramid, progressing from knowledge recall to dynamic case management. On the computational side, we link deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning patterns to common medical goals and tasks. We also introduce a benchmark dataset spanning five levels of medical reasoning capability and report results on 18 state-of-the-art models, revealing that medical specialist models excel in diagnosis-centric tasks while general models lead in decision support and dialogue. We conclude by discussing current progress and open challenges, including data limitations, hallucination, and grounding issues, and outline directions toward safer, more reliable, and workflow-ready systems.

</details>


### [64] [Alignment Plausibility: A New Standard for Assuring AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07766)
*Gwydion Williams,Sara Zannone,Bilal A Mateen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"对齐合理性"（alignment plausibility）的监管框架，通过类比临床实践的三层安全保证机制（明确的价值规范、嵌入价值的训练、部署中的监督），来解决大型语言模型在心理健康支持中因注意力经济导向而产生的隐性长期风险（如依赖、边界侵蚀、信念扭曲放大等问题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在心理健康支持中面临注意力经济驱动的运营目标与有效心理支持所需"摩擦"之间的矛盾，开发者的安全应对主要针对显性急性伤害，而忽视了依赖、边界侵蚀、扭曲信念放大等隐性长期风险模式。

Method: 提出三层对齐组织架构：1）基于临床实践规范承诺的显式价值规范；2）将这些价值嵌入模型的训练；3）部署中类似临床督导的漂移与长期伤害监测。由此构建"对齐合理性"概念，类比生物学合理性作为AI健康领域的监管构念。

Result: 形成了"对齐合理性"这一结构化论证框架，用于系统性地论证AI系统在价值观、训练机制和监督机制上是否一致地指向安全与积极的健康结果。

Conclusion: 对齐合理性可作为健康AI领域论证系统是否真正对齐积极健康结果、在有能力造成伤害时是否不会造成伤害、以及最终是否使患者受益的监管原则。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become significant providers of mental health support, yet they remain products of an attention economy whose operational and commercial targets favour sustained engagement over the friction that effective psychological support often requires. Developers' safety responses have been largely reactive, addressing the most visible and acute harms while subtler, longer-term patterns of risk (e.g., dependency, boundary erosion, the amplification of distorted beliefs) receive less attention. We contend that making LLMs structurally safe requires alignment organised at three levels that mirror how society assures the safety of human clinical practice: 1) explicit value specification grounded in the codified normative commitments of clinical practice; 2) training that embeds those values in the model; and 3) oversight that detects drift and longer-term harm during deployment, much as clinical supervision does for human practice. Organising alignment in this way yields a construct we call alignment plausibility - a structured demonstration that a system's values, training regime, and oversight mechanisms are together consistent with safe and positive outcomes. We propose alignment plausibility as a regulatory construct (by drawing analogy to the established construct of biological plausibility) for AI in health: a principled way to argue for, or against, trust that systems are aligned to positive health outcomes, will cause no harm even where capable of doing so, and will ultimately lead to patient benefit.

</details>


### [65] [Idiobionics: The Unification of Privacy and Intelligent Robotic Prostheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07775)
*Kwesi Afari Darfoor,Patrick M. Pilarski,Bailey Kacsmar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“idiobionics”这一新研究领域，系统探讨智能仿生肢体的隐私风险问题。作者论证了先进的传感与AI控制技术虽提升了假肢性能，但也引入了隐私威胁向量，并初步展示了潜在对抗性攻击的证据，最后提出了该领域的关键开放研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能仿生肢体（机器人假肢）的传感和控制技术进步在提升能力的同时，也引入了可能被恶意利用的隐私威胁向量，而这些问题尚未被充分研究。为了充分实现下一代仿生肢体的益处，必须直接理解和解决这些隐私风险及其对用户接受度造成的障碍。

Method: 通过定义新研究领域“idiobionics”，将其扎根于相关文献综述，提供初步证据展示和讨论可能利用智能仿生肢体设计的对抗性攻击，并整理出一系列面向可穿戴机器人和其他人机交互自主系统研究者的开放研究问题。

Result: 初步展示了潜在对抗性攻击的证据，证明智能仿生肢体的设计可被恶意利用；梳理了该领域相关的开放研究问题清单。

Conclusion: Idiobionics研究将有助于释放机器人假肢及相关仿生设备的全部潜力，需在技术发展的同时系统性地解决隐私安全问题。

Abstract: The human body is at the center of a growing family of technologies designed to tightly and persistently couple biological and digital systems. Robotic prostheses are a representative example of this tight coupling. Also referred to as bionic limbs, robotic prostheses are devices that support people who have lost limbs in pursuing daily life activities such as walking and grasping objects. Bionic limbs are now perceptive and responsive owing to their integration with advanced sensors and artificial intelligence-based control approaches. Consequently, such robotic prostheses can now be viewed as semiautonomous wearable robotic systems that can co-adapt with their users. However, the same sensing and control advancements that increase the capability of robotic prostheses also introduce threat vectors that could be exploited by malicious entities to violate the privacy of users. To fully realize the benefits of next-generation bionic limbs, we maintain it is important to directly understand and address these privacy risks and the barriers they might present to user adoption. This paper therefore introduces a new line of inquiry we term idiobionics to holistically investigate issues at the intersection of privacy and intelligent bionic limbs. As the main contribution of this paper, we define idiobionics, ground it in related literature, and provide preliminary evidence showing and discussing potential adversarial attacks that could exploit intelligent bionic limb designs. We then contribute a curated list of open research questions within idiobionics that are relevant to researchers in wearable robotics and other human-facing autonomous systems. We expect that idiobionics research will help unlock the full potential of robotic prostheses and related bionic devices.

</details>


### [66] [Infinity-Parser2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07836)
*Zuming Huang,Jun Huang,Kexuan Ren,Baode Wang,Weizhen Li,Jianming Feng,Yu Wang,Yichen Yao,Shijun Lin,Yige Tang,Cheng Peng,Weidi Xu,Wei Chu,Yinghui Xu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Infinity-Parser2 是一个大型多模态模型，通过可控数据合成流水线和多任务强化学习实现端到端文档解析，开源了包含500万样本的双语文档语料库 Infinity-Doc2-5M，并发布两个变体：Flash（低延迟）和 Pro（高精度），在多个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决文档解析领域中忠实标注的解析语料长期稀缺的问题，现有数据难以满足高精度文档解析需求。

Method: 构建可控数据合成引擎（可渲染框架+迭代优化循环），生成500万双语样本语料库；设计可验证的多任务奖励系统，在8个联合训练目标上实施联合强化学习；基于共享架构发布 Flash（低延迟）和 Pro（高精度）两个变体。

Result: Infinity-Parser2-Pro 在 olmOCR-Bench 上达到87.6%，ParseBench 上达到74.3%，超越 DeepSeek-OCR-2、PaddleOCR-VL-1.5 和 MinerU2.5；Flash 变体吞吐量是 Infinity-Parser-7B 的3.68倍。

Conclusion: 提出了一种结合可控数据合成与多任务强化学习的有效范式，在文档解析及相关任务上取得 SOTA 结果，并展现出对图表、化学公式和文档 VQA 的强泛化能力。

Abstract: We present Infinity-Parser2, a large multimodal model that couples a controllable data-synthesis pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning for end-to-end document parsing, addressing the persistent scarcity of faithfully annotated parsing corpora. Our contributions are threefold. First, we build a scalable synthesis engine, pairing a controllable rendering framework with an iterative refinement loop, and use it to construct and open-source Infinity-Doc2-5M: a 5-million-sample bilingual (Chinese/English) corpus spanning diverse document types, annotated with element bounding boxes, canonical content forms (Markdown, HTML, LaTeX, SMILES, structured charts), and full-page reading order. Second, we introduce a verifiable, multi-task reward system that enables Joint Reinforcement Learning across eight co-trained objectives (document parsing, layout analysis, table parsing, math formula parsing, chart parsing, chemical formula parsing, document VQA, and general multimodal understanding), unifying perception, structure, and reasoning in a single optimization signal. Third, we release two variants under a shared architecture: Infinity-Parser2-Flash, optimized for low-latency inference with a $3.68\times$ throughput gain over Infinity-Parser-7B, and Infinity-Parser2-Pro, engineered for precision-critical settings. Infinity-Parser2-Pro reaches state-of-the-art 87.6% on olmOCR-Bench and 74.3% on ParseBench, surpassing DeepSeek-OCR-2, PaddleOCR-VL-1.5, and MinerU2.5, with strong generalization to charts, chemical formulas, and document VQA.

</details>


### [67] [VectorizationLLM: Smart Vectorization Based AI Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07846)
*Ryan Duke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VectorizationLLM是一个基于Google开源权重的专用大型语言模型，用于辅助学生通过RAG知识库和系统提示架构学习MATLAB中的向量化、时/波矢量分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程，作为指导性助教提供概念讲解和课堂笔记示例，但不提供直接答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个专用的教学辅助LLM，帮助学生掌握计算分析课程中复杂的数学和编程概念，同时避免直接给出答案以促进深层学习。

Method: 基于Google开源权重LLM进行微调，采用RAG（检索增强生成）知识库和系统提示架构，在回答中整合代码、文本和图像示例，并设计为仅提供概念讲解和课堂笔记示例而不直接回答问题。

Result: 开发了VectorizationLLM模型，应用于NYIT Old Westbury电气与计算机工程技术系的CTEC 247课程，覆盖向量化、时/波矢量分析、分段函数、傅里叶分析和微分方程等主题。

Conclusion: VectorizationLLM作为一种指导性助教工具，通过结构化知识库和示例驱动的方式，在MATLAB计算分析教学中发挥辅助作用。

Abstract: VectorizationLLM is a specialized Large Language Model based on Google open-weight LLMs. The model is designed to assist students to learn smart vectorization, time/wave vector analysis, piecewise functions, Fourier analysis, and differential equations in MATLAB. The course application is CTEC 247: Applied Computational Analysis II by the Department of Electrical & Computer Engineering Technology at New York Institute of Technology Old Westbury. The LLM model is designed to be an instructive assistant, providing detailed explanations of concepts with examples from in-class notes without providing direct answers to questions. The model is designed with a RAG (Retrieval Augmented Generation) knowledge base and system prompt architecture. Examples in both code, text, and images are provided in the LLM responses.

</details>


### [68] [A Graph Neural Network Model for Real-Time Gesture Recognition Based on sEMG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07850)
*Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Manohar Murthi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络的表面肌电信号(sEMG)表征方法，利用从Myoband采集的8通道sEMG信号构建肌肉激活模式图网络，实现了99%的手势识别准确率，平均处理时间48ms，适用于实时应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现高级假肢和增强现实的无缝控制，需要准确且即时的手势识别。传统sEMG处理方法在实时性和准确性方面存在局限，亟需新的表征方法。

Method: 利用图网络构建前臂肌肉激活模式的sEMG表征，基于该图网络设计图神经网络(GNN)算法进行实时手势识别。使用Myoband（8个电极）采集8名健康受试者的sEMG信号进行性能评估。

Result: 平均分类准确率达到99%，超越了现有最佳技术。在M1 Pro CPU上，图构建和预测的平均时间为48ms。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于图网络的sEMG表征方法在准确性和实时性方面均表现优异，非常适合实时手势识别应用场景。

Abstract: For seemless control of advanced hand prostheses and augmented reality, accurate and immediate hand gestures recognition is essential. Surface electromyography (sEMG) signals obtained from the forearm are commonly employed for this purpose. In this paper, we present a novel approach for sEMG representation that utilizes graph networks which contain information about muscle activation patterns in the forearm. Based on these graph networks, we have developed a machine learning algorithm capable of real-time hand gesture recognition using a graph neural network. The algorithm's performance was evaluated using sEMG signals acquired from myoband, which has 8 electrodes placed around the forearm, involving 8 healthy subjects. The proposed method demonstrated an average classification accuracy of 99\%, surpassing the performance of state-of-the-art techniques. The average time for both graph construction and prediction stood at 48ms utilizing a M1 pro CPU, rendering the approach well-suited for real-time applications.

</details>


### [69] [Agentic AI and Retrieval-Augmented Models in Straight-Through Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07858)
*Robert Richardson,Josh Meyers,Brian Hartman,David Sandberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了从传统规则自动化到大语言模型、RAG及多智能体系统等AI架构如何支持精算实务中的透明度、可审计性和人机协同治理，并通过构建面向小型商业企业保单的自动化核保框架，证明多智能体RAG系统在多步骤和缺失信息场景下表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI正在重塑精算实务，尤其在处理非结构化文档、异构数据源和受监管决策流程方面。精算师面临从传统规则自动化到LLM、RAG和多智能体系统的广泛设计空间，亟需系统比较这些新兴架构在透明度、可审计性和人机协同治理方面的表现。

Method: 构建合成但逼真的实验环境，比较三种核保流水线：(i) 单LLM基线、(ii) 朴素RAG系统、(iii) 多智能体"Agentic RAG"流水线，后者结合了定向检索、第三方数据检查和显式多步规则评估。

Result: 智能体系统整体表现最优，在多步骤和缺失信息场景下增益最大，结构化的检索和反思帮助模型避免无依据的直通决策。

Conclusion: 多智能体RAG架构能更好地支持精算领域的合规和治理需求，尤其适用于需要多步推理和处理信息不完整场景的直通式核保决策流程。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is beginning to reshape actuarial practice, particularly in domains that require reasoning over unstructured documents, heterogeneous data sources, and regulated decision workflows. Actuaries now face a design space that ranges from traditional rule-based automation to large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and multi-agent ``agentic'' systems that plan, retrieve, call tools, and reflect. This paper examines how these emerging architectures can support actuarial priorities such as transparency, auditability, and human-in-the-loop governance, with a focus on straight-through decision processes. To make these ideas concrete, we develop and analyze an agentic AI framework for straight-through underwriting of small commercial Business Owner Policies (BOPs). We construct a synthetic but realistic experimental environment and compare three underwriting pipelines: (i) a single-LLM baseline, (ii) a naive RAG system, and (iii) a multi-agent ``Agentic RAG'' pipeline that combines targeted retrieval, third-party data checks, and explicit multi-step rule evaluation. The agentic system performs best overall, with the largest gains in multi-step and missing-information scenarios, where structured retrieval and reflection help the model avoid unsupported straight-through decisions.

</details>


### [70] [Feedback Manipulation Regularization: Enabling Offline Agent Alignment for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07859)
*Benjamin Poole,Minwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出反馈操纵正则化（FMR），一种算法无关的方法，利用评估反馈作为纠偏信号来改进模仿学习策略的对齐性，在安全健身房环境中实现高达98%的失调减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要将人类演示和反馈结合为多阶段流水线，适用于上下文强盗框架下的语言生成，但缺乏在完全序列决策环境中将这两种互补信号作为单阶段离线训练中更丰富互联信号的研究。

Method: 提出反馈操纵正则化（FMR），一种算法无关的方法，将评估反馈作为纠偏信号来改进模仿学习策略的对齐性，并适配Safety Gymnasium环境作为对齐评估的测试基准。

Result: 在多种模仿学习算法上展示了对齐能力的提升，失调减少高达98%，且在数据有限（包括稀疏对齐和无信息噪声演示）的情况下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FMR能够有效利用评估反馈作为纠偏信号，在完全序列决策环境中实现单阶段离线训练，显著提升模仿学习策略的对齐性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) research has increasingly shifted focus towards alignment, ensuring agents learn behaviors adhering to human values. While human demonstrations and feedback have proven crucial for alignment, existing approaches predominantly combine these signals using multi-stage pipelines designed for the contextual bandit framing of language generation. Yet little work explores how these complementary inputs can serve as a richer, interconnected signal for single-stage offline training in fully sequential decision-making environments. We propose Feedback Manipulation Regularization (FMR), an algorithm-agnostic method that harnesses evaluative feedback as a corrective signal to improve the alignment of imitation learning policies. We adapt Safety Gymnasium environments to be a principled testbed for alignment evaluation, demonstrating improved aptitude and up to a 98\% reduction in misalignment across a range of imitation learning algorithms. FMR remains robust in limited data regimes, even when learning from scarce aligned and uninformative noisy demonstrations.

</details>


### [71] [Nigeria Machinery: A Low-Resource Industrial Dataset with a Domain-Grounded Reasoning Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07883)
*Gospel Bassey,Vincent Fakiyesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发布了尼日利亚机械使用与故障数据集（89条机器记录，28个指标，覆盖2006-2025年制造业和油气行业），并开发了一种从稀疏数值中构建思维链推理示例的方法。该数据适配工作解决了语言模型构建数据集时提示与真实领域脱节的问题，使领域关联提示从1/78提升至94/94，检索答案准确率达84/84。数据集以CC-BY-4.0协议发布，但受限于89条记录和17个单观测指标，主要作为参考和种子数据集使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲经济体工业机械数据稀缺，缺乏公开且可直接用于建模的数据，导致难以进行定量分析或训练语言模型处理基于该场景的数值任务。

Method: 1) 构建尼日利亚机械使用与故障数据集，包含89条机器记录、28个指标，覆盖制造业和油气行业，每条记录标注公开来源并由代码本解码。2) 开发从稀疏数值中构建思维链推理示例的方法，生成94条提示-补全-推理轨迹数据。3) 识别并解决语言模型构建数据集时提示与真实领域脱节的问题。

Result: 领域关联提示从早期版本的1/78提升至94/94；检索答案匹配源值率达84/84；数据集以CC-BY-4.0协议发布，包含数据、推理层和每行的溯源文件。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了非洲工业机械领域的参考和种子数据集，虽规模有限（89条记录，17个单观测指标），推理行多为检索而非多步计算，但为后续研究奠定了基础。数据适配工作由Adaption Labs完成。

Abstract: There is relatively little, public, and model-ready data on industrial machinery for African economies. This makes it hard to do quantitative analysis or to train language models on numeric tasks grounded in that setting. We release two things to help with part of this problem. The first is the Nigeria Machinery Usage and Failures Dataset: 89 machine-level records across 28 indicators, covering Nigeria's manufacturing and oil and gas sectors from 2006 to 2025. Every record names a public source and is decoded by a codebook. The second is a method for building chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning examples from these sparse numeric values. The result is 94 prompt, completion, and reasoning-trace rows. In every row, the prompt names the real indicator, subsector, year, and source of the record it comes from. The data adaptation work was carried out by Adaption Labs. Along the way we describe a problem that is common when language models are used to build datasets. The prompts can match the real numbers while saying nothing about the real domain. We show that fixing this raises the share of domain-grounded prompts from 1 out of 78 in an earlier release to 94 out of 94, and that every retrieval answer now matches its source value (84 out of 84). We release the data, the reasoning layer, and a per-row provenance file under CC-BY-4.0. We are clear about the limits. With 89 records and 17 indicators that have only one observation, this is a reference and seed dataset, not a large training set. Most reasoning rows are retrieval rather than multi-step computation.

</details>


### [72] [A variational autoencoder approach for choice set generation and implicit perception of alternatives in choice modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2106.13319)
*Rui Yao,Shlomo Bekhor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有隐含可用性/感知（IAP）的广义极值（GEV）模型，并提出了一种变分自编码器（VAE）方法用于选择集生成和替代方案的隐含感知。以交叉巢式Logit（CNL）模型作为IAP-GEV模型的实例，通过真实数据集验证了该方法在路径选择集生成中的有效性。与多项Logit模型和传统选择集生成方法相比，估计的IAP-CNL模型在拟合优度和预测性能上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统选择集生成方法通常假设决策者对所有替代方案具有完全感知，这与实际决策行为不符。本文旨在解决选择集隐含感知问题，通过GEV框架和VAE方法更好地模拟真实选择过程，提高模型预测准确性。

Method: 1) 推导了具有IAP的GEV模型理论框架；2) 以CNL模型为例构建了IAP-GEV模型；3) 采用VAE方法建模选择集生成过程，最大化选择集中被选替代方案的感知似然；4) 利用真实路径选择数据集进行实证验证。

Result: IAP-CNL模型在拟合优度和预测性能上均优于多项Logit模型及传统选择集生成方法，表明VAE方法能够有效捕捉决策者的隐含感知过程，生成更符合实际的选择集。

Conclusion: VAE方法为选择集生成和隐含感知建模提供了一种有效的新途径，IAP-GEV模型框架能够显著提升离散选择模型的实证表现，尤其适用于路径选择等复杂决策场景。

Abstract: This paper derives the generalized extreme value (GEV) model with implicit availability/perception (IAP) of alternatives and proposes a variational autoencoder (VAE) approach for choice set generation and implicit perception of alternatives. Specifically, the cross-nested logit (CNL) model with IAP is derived as an example of IAP-GEV models. The VAE approach is adapted to model the choice set generation process, in which the likelihood of perceiving chosen alternatives in the choice set is maximized. The VAE approach for route choice set generation is exemplified using a real dataset. IAP- CNL model estimated has the best performance in terms of goodness-of-fit and prediction performance, compared to multinomial logit models and conventional choice set generation methods.

</details>


### [73] [Persona Cartography: Charting Language Model Personality Traits in Weight Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07916)
*Luke Baines,Anton Gonzalvez Hawthorne,Mariia Koroliuk,Irakli Shalibashvili,Clément Dumas,Konstantinos Voudouris,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用OCEAN人格框架（开放性、尽责性、外倾性、宜人性、神经质）对大型语言模型的行为特质进行建模，通过低秩适配器（LoRA）实现人格特质的放大或抑制，并验证了这些特质轴对模型安全行为的影响。研究还提出一种无监督心理测量流程，从模型输出中恢复了四个可解释的行为因子（语气、主动性、说教性、认知谨慎）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型表现出反复出现的行为模式（即人格），这些模式影响泛化和安全性，但目前缺乏可靠的工具来分解、测量和控制这些行为模式。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 采用OCEAN框架将模型人格描述为行为特质空间中的位置；2) 训练低秩适配器（LoRA）以放大或抑制个体特质；3) 使用LLM评估器（经人类验证面板校准）、特质特异性多项选择基准和标准能力评估进行效果评估；4) 提出无监督心理测量流程从模型输出中恢复行为因子。

Result: 1) 每个适配器在目标特质上大致单调移动，且效果随规模增大而增强；2) 多个适配器可近似加性组合以构建混合人格；3) 在中等规模下保持能力基准性能；4) 诱导的特质轴影响下游安全相关行为（如神经质影响挫败感，宜人性影响谄媚）；5) 无监督流程恢复了四个可解释的行为因子。

Conclusion: 人格控制可以理解为在权重空间中学习、缩放和组合特质，为人格测量、模型编辑和安全性之间架起了桥梁。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit recurring behavioural patterns -- personas -- that shape generalisation and safety, but we lack reliable tools for decomposing, measuring, and controlling them. Our central insight is to treat personas as positions in a space of behavioural traits, using the OCEAN framework to describe model personas in terms of Openness, Conscientiousness, Extraversion, Agreeableness, and Neuroticism. We train low-rank adapters to amplify or suppress individual traits, and evaluate their effects using an LLM-judge calibrated against a human-validated panel, trait-specific multiple-choice benchmarks, and standard capability evaluations. Across six models from three families (4B-32B), we find that each adapter moves its target trait largely monotonically with scale, combines approximately additively with other adapters to construct mixed personas, and preserves performance on capability benchmarks at moderate scales. We further show that the induced trait axes affect safety-relevant behaviour in downstream evaluations: for example, moving along neuroticism and agreeableness axes affects frustration and sycophancy respectively. We also introduce an unsupervised psychometric pipeline that recovers four interpretable behavioural factors (tone, initiative, didacticism, epistemic caution) from model rollouts. Persona control can then be considered in terms of learning, scaling, and composing traits in weight space, providing a bridge between personality measurement, model editing, and safety.

</details>


### [74] [Evaluating the Effect of Frame Rate in Sequence-Based Classification of Autism-Related Self-Stimulatory Hand Idiosyncrasies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07957)
*Raunak Mondal,Peter Washington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究针对自闭症自我刺激行为的视频自动检测，对比了LSTM和GRU模型在不同帧采样率下的表现（最优15帧间隔，GRU达98.75%准确率），并系统评估了十种数据增强策略，发现水平翻转效果最佳（48.78%），而欠采样不可或缺。个性化机器学习方法（逐主体时序分割）获得一致的预测结果（平均损失1.84，SD 0.79）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过7500万人患有自闭症谱系障碍，但目前缺乏可扩展的远程行为筛查计算方法。本文旨在解决视频中自闭症相关自我刺激行为自动检测的两个互补性挑战：最优序列神经网络架构与时序采样率的选择，以及针对小规模行为数据集的数据增强策略。

Method: 使用SSBD数据集，基于姿态特征训练LSTM和GRU模型，在6种帧采样间隔（1,5,15,30,45,90帧）下比较性能；在I3D迁移学习流程中应用十种数据增强策略并开展消融研究；采用个性化机器学习（逐主体模型，时序分割训练/测试）。

Result: LSTM和GRU均超越CNN基线（62-76%），峰值准确率分别为97.5%（LSTM）和98.75%（GRU），最优采样间隔为15帧。水平翻转增强独立准确率最高（48.78%），移除欠采样导致最大性能下降。个性化模型平均损失1.84，SD 0.79。

Conclusion: 为数据稀缺的临床视频行为分类任务提供了架构选择、采样率和增强策略的具体实践指导。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) affects over 75 million individuals worldwide, yet scalable computational methods for remote behavioral screening remain limited. This study addresses two complementary challenges in automated detection of autism-related self-stimulatory behaviors from video: (1) identifying the optimal sequence-based neural network architecture and temporal sampling rate, and (2) characterizing data augmentation strategies for training on small behavioral datasets. For the first objective, long short-term memory (LSTM) and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models were trained on pose-derived features from the Self-Stimulatory Behavior Diagnosis (SSBD) dataset at frame sampling intervals of 1, 5, 15, 30, 45, and 90 frames. Both architectures exceeded prior convolutional neural network (CNN) baselines (62-76% accuracy), with peak accuracies of 97.5% (LSTM) and 98.75% (GRU) at a sampling interval of every 15 frames. For the second objective, ten data augmentation strategies were applied to an I3D transfer learning pipeline, with an ablation study quantifying the marginal contribution of each technique. Horizontal flip achieved the highest standalone accuracy (48.78%), while exclusion of upsampling from the augmentation pipeline produced the largest performance degradation, indicating its necessity for complex behavioral video augmentation. A personalized machine learning approach, in which per-subject models were trained and tested on temporally split segments of each video, produced consistent predictions (mean loss 1.84, SD 0.79). These results provide practitioners with concrete guidance on architecture selection, sampling rate, and augmentation strategy for video-based behavioral classification in data-scarce clinical domains.

</details>


### [75] [Agentic Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07984)
*Seokhoon Jeong,Mijung Kim,Taehwan Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentNAS 提出一种结合 LLM 与 NAS 的机制：LLM 生成高质量种子架构并分解为带命名可替换模块的"槽位架构"，自动定义有界且任务特化的搜索空间供传统 NAS 探索，无需人工构建。在 17 个任务上取得 11 个 SOTA，消融实验证明两者互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NAS 方法受限于人工设计的搜索空间，需要大量领域知识且每次新任务需重建；LLM 虽可在开放空间生成架构，但如何最优分配 LLM 驱动设计与 NAS 驱动搜索之间的分工尚未被探索。

Method: 提出一种桥接 LLM 与 NAS 的机制：LLM 生成高质量种子架构，然后将其分解为"槽位架构"（slotted architecture）——一个带有命名、可互换模块槽的脚手架，自动定义有界且任务特化的搜索空间供传统 NAS 探索。实例化为 AgentNAS，一个模块化三阶段流水线，各组件贡献可独立衡量。

Result: 在涵盖分类、密集回归、分割、多标签标注等 17 个任务上（NAS-Bench-360 和 Unseen NAS），AgentNAS 在 11 个任务上达到新 SOTA，超越包括任务特化专家设计在内的已发表基线。消融实验显示：LLM 生成的种子已在多数任务上超越基线，NAS 通过跨槽位的组合重组在大多数情况下带来额外增益——这种搜索模式独立 LLM 采样无法复制。三种不同能力水平的 LLM 均表现出一致模式。

Conclusion: LLM 与 NAS 的劳动力分工是鲁棒且互补的：LLM 负责产生高质量种子，NAS 负责跨槽位的组合搜索，两者结合可自动构建高性能任务特化架构，无需人工工程。

Abstract: Neural architecture search (NAS) methods have grown increasingly efficient, yet they remain bounded by manually engineered search spaces that require substantial domain expertise and must be rebuilt for every new task. Large language models (LLMs) can generate architectures in an open-ended space, but how to optimally divide the labor between LLM-driven design and NAS-driven search remains unexplored. We propose a mechanism that bridges these two paradigms: an LLM produces a high-quality seed architecture, then decomposes it into a "slotted architecture", a scaffold with named, interchangeable module slots that automatically defines a bounded, task-specific search space for conventional NAS to explore, without manual engineering. We instantiate this mechanism in AgentNAS, a modular three-phase pipeline in which each component's contribution can be measured independently. On 17 tasks spanning classification, dense regression, segmentation, and multi-label tagging across diverse modalities (NAS-Bench-360 and Unseen NAS), AgentNAS establishes a new state of the art on 11 tasks, outperforming published baselines including task-specific expert designs. Ablation studies show that the two search mechanisms are broadly complementary: the LLM-generated seed already surpasses published baselines on the majority of tasks, and NAS delivers additional gains in most cases through combinatorial recombination across slots, a mode of search that independent LLM samples cannot replicate. These patterns hold across three LLMs of different capability levels, confirming that the division of labor is robust. Our code is available at https://github.com/alroimfebruary/AgentNAS.

</details>


### [76] [Concretized Proposition Prompting Resolves Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08018)
*Changhun Lee,Minguk Jeon,Jongkyung Shin,Chiehyeon Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出具体化命题提示（CPP）框架，通过显式具体化与问题相关的命题，解决LLM在组合性与知识性之间的二象性困境，在医疗和数学基准上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在推理时经常难以平衡组合性（compositionality）与知识性（knowledgeability），即组合-知识二象性（Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy）问题。

Method: 提出具体化命题提示（Concretized Proposition Prompting, CPP）框架，显式地将与问题相关的命题进行具体化处理。

Result: CPP显著提升了推理性能，在医疗基准（精确知识优先）上表现尤为突出，在数学基准（演绎推理优先）上也具有竞争力；CPP可扩展到多种基础模型和参数规模。

Conclusion: CPP解决了组合-知识二象性，为逻辑组织化和事实基础化的推理提供了坚实基础，是一种弥合基于组合与基于知识方法之间差距的基本范式。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle to balance compositionality with knowledgeability, a challenge we define as Composition-Knowledge Dichotomy. To address this, we propose Concretized Proposition Prompting (CPP), a framework that explicitly concretizes propositions relevant to questions. The results demonstrate that CPP significantly enhances reasoning performance, particularly in medical benchmarks where precise knowledge is paramount, while being competitive on math benchmarks where deductive reasoning is prioritized. Additional experiments reveal that CPP is scalable to various foundation models and parameter sizes, being a fundamental paradigm that bridges the gap between composition- and knowledge-based approaches. Consequently, CPP resolves the composition-knowledge dichotomy by providing a solid foundation for logically organized and factually grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [77] [From Prompts to Contracts: Harness Engineering for Auditable Enterprise LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08028)
*Joongho Ahn,Moonsoo Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"工具化工程"方法，将企业LLM应用从原型阶段（依赖提示词和检索上下文）转化为可追溯、可审计的LLM-agent架构。该方法将确定性行为转移到代码、清单、模式和验证工件中，并在韩国25家上市公司的数据上验证了其保持源 grounding、实体路由、输出卫生和推荐语言契约的能力。实验表明，该架构在模型替换、故障注入等场景下均能有效执行契约，且代码级别的保障无法仅通过提示词实现，同时在安全性和实用性上优于外部护栏方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级LLM应用从原型转化为产品化时，面临源边界、实体路由、答案契约和可追溯性等需求，而现有基于提示词和检索上下文的方法难以满足这些要求。

Method: 提出工具化工程方法，将确定性行为从LLM迁移到代码、清单、模式和验证工件中，构建围绕可替换组合边界的架构，使源支持的主张保持对运行时答案的权威性。

Result: (1) 在所有验证场景中保持了源 grounding 等契约，故障注入控制确认验证器能标记故意破坏的契约。(2) 三个托管模型共270次组合边界运行全部通过检查。(3) 仅靠提示词无法实现代码级保障，外部护栏虽能阻止违规但过度拒绝（88/120），而工具化架构保持全效用（120/120）。

Conclusion: 该模式是一种可复用的工程范式，能将探索性原型转化为具有版本化源、控制和验证工件的可审计应用。

Abstract: Enterprise large language model (LLM) applications often begin as prototypes whose behavior is carried by prompts and retrieval context. Productization adds requirements for source boundaries, entity routing, answer contracts, and reproducible traces. We present a harness-engineering approach that reconstructs this pattern into a traceable, auditable LLM-agent architecture: deterministic behavior moves into code, manifests, schemas, and validation artifacts around a replaceable composition boundary, while source-backed claims remain the authority for runtime answers. We instantiate it on a public-data slice of five Korean corporate groups (25 listed companies) and evaluate three research questions. (1) The harness preserves its source-grounding, entity-routing, trace, output-hygiene, and recommendation-language contracts across the fixed validation scenarios; a fault-injection control confirms the validators flag deliberately broken contracts. (2) The checks the harness enforces held under model substitution: across three hosted models, they passed on all 270 composition-boundary runs; failures were confined to the model-composed side and were caught and recorded. (3) The code-owned guarantees are load-bearing, not reproducible by prompting alone: holding the model fixed and varying only the enforcement layer, prompt instructions alone let recommendation-language and internal-trace-leakage violations reach the reader, which the harness blocks entirely. A bolt-on external guardrail prevents such violations too but over-refuses, dropping utility to 88/120 where the harness preserves full utility (120/120); in this ablation, only code-owned enforcement preserves both safety and utility. The result is a reusable engineering pattern for turning exploratory prototypes into auditable applications with versioned source, control, and validation artifacts.

</details>


### [78] [A safety-oriented hypothetico-deductive framework for AI-assisted differential diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08038)
*Fan Ma,Mauro Giuffrè,Donald Wright,Kent McCann,Mark Iscoe,Lingfei Qian,Mingyang Jiang,Chi Wing Ng,Na Hong,Huan He,Cathy Shyr,Qingyu Chen,Lee Schwamm,Lucila Ohno-Machado,Hua Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AegisDx，一个面向安全的假设演绎临床推理框架，通过角色分工、结构化中间输出、证据检索接口和验证门控机制，提升LLM在诊断任务中的安全性和准确性。在多项医学案例和真实急诊病历评估中，AegisDx在Top-3诊断准确率、必须排查疾病捕获率和医生评估的安全评分上均显著优于基线和独立LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM诊断系统通常将诊断视为一次性预测任务，缺乏对遗漏高风险替代诊断的防护机制，也缺少对推理论证的严格验证，存在患者安全隐患。

Method: AegisDx通过以下机制实现安全导向的临床推理：1) 角色特定的合约协调多个专业LLM组件；2) 结构化中间输出；3) 证据检索接口；4) 验证门控，用于生成宽覆盖鉴别诊断、强制执行"必须排查"危险疾病的筛查、基于医学证据验证推理、构建可操作的后续步骤。

Result: 在NEJM/JAMA案例中，AegisDx的Top-3准确率比独立LLM提升约10个百分点；在急诊医学案例中Top-3准确率85.7% vs 68.6%；必须排查疾病捕获率78.0% vs 52.0%；在Yale New Haven Health真实急诊病历的盲法评估中，医师评定的综合安全评分从4.31提升至4.55（5分制，p=2.1×10⁻⁴）。

Conclusion: 将诊断AI工程化为安全导向的推理框架，而非仅优化原始预测准确性，能够为急性护理工作流程提供更安全、更透明且临床有意义的床旁决策支持。

Abstract: Diagnostic error is a major threat to patient safety, yet current large language model (LLM) systems often treat diagnosis as a one-shot prediction task, lacking safeguards against missed high-risk alternatives or rigorous verification of their reasoning. Here, we present AegisDx, a safety-oriented framework for hypothetico-deductive clinical reasoning. AegisDx coordinates specialized LLM components through role-specific contracts, structured intermediate outputs, evidence-retrieval interfaces, and verification gates to generate broad differential diagnoses, enforce explicit screening for dangerous "must-not-miss" conditions, verify reasoning against grounded medical evidence, and structure actionable next steps. We evaluated AegisDx across three layers. On literature-derived case reports from NEJM and JAMA, with GPT-oss-120B as the shared backbone, Top-3 diagnostic accuracy was 59.9% versus 52.1% for the standalone LLM on JAMA cases and 62.7% versus 51.4% on NEJM cases. On cases from Annals of Emergency Medicine, Top-3 accuracy was 85.7% versus 68.6%; against physician-consensus must-not-miss diagnosis sets, AegisDx captured at least one such condition among its top three diagnoses in 78.0% of cases versus 52.0%. In a blinded physician evaluation of 43 real-world emergency department notes from the Yale New Haven Health System compared against GPT-5, AegisDx improved the physician-rated composite safety score from 4.31 to 4.55 on a 5-point scale (adjusted p = 2.1x10^-4), with qualitative gains in must-not-miss identification and reasoning safety. Our findings suggest that engineering diagnostic AI as a safety-oriented reasoning framework, rather than optimizing raw predictive accuracy alone, can provide a safer, more transparent, and clinically meaningful layer of bedside decision support for acute care workflows.

</details>


### [79] [When LLMs Agree, Are They Right? Auditing Self-Consistency and Cross-Model Agreement as Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08065)
*Kaihua Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了LLM-as-judge评估范式中“一致性等于正确性”的假设不可靠，并通过大规模跨实验研究发现，一致性仅在特定条件下（如中档模型、计算分配）才是正确性的弱代理指标，而前沿模型的高一致性反而伴随着高错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-judge评估体系（包括集成评估和混合专家面板）普遍默认一致性（评估者间或模型自采样间的同意程度）等同于正确性，这一假设缺乏验证。作者旨在检验该假设的可靠性，并探索一致性在何种条件下可以作为正确性的可用代理。

Method: 开展大规模跨实验者研究：53名实验者对重叠案例进行K=50次采样，覆盖模型层级、提示策略、规模等变量，在GPQA Diamond和AIME基准上生成265,000个样本。使用多数正确作为部署标签，采用层次化实验者聚类bootstrap进行统计分析，并进行跨模型家族的验证。

Result: 一致性是正确性的正但弱预测因子（rho 0.20-0.59）；其效用具条件依赖性：对未饱和的中档模型和计算分配场景最佳，而对最一致的前沿模型最差——该模型在GPQA 77%的条目中一致性≥0.8，其中48%是错误的。跨模型家族验证确认了前沿模型的过度自信，错误模式具有跨提供商的系统性。

Conclusion: 自一致性仅是正确性的条件代理，不能作为独立的置信度分数使用。论文公开了去标识化的实验数据以供进一步研究。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge (Zheng et al., 2023) is increasingly the default for evaluating AI systems in enterprise pipelines, often scaled to ensembles (Verga et al., 2024) or "mixture-of-experts" (Shazeer et al., 2017) panels of judges. These systems share a key assumption: that consistency -- agreement among judges, or among a model's own samples -- indicates correctness. We show this assumption is unreliable. Agreement is not accuracy: a model can agree with itself, and different models can agree with each other, out of shared bias, a memorized heuristic, or an option-position prior rather than truth. We ask when agreement is nonetheless a usable proxy, in a large-scale cross-runner study: 53 runners drew K=50 samples for assigned overlapping cases across comparisons of model tier, prompting, and scale on GPQA Diamond and AIME -- 265,000 samples. Using majority-correctness as the deployment label and a hierarchical runner-clustered bootstrap, agreement is a positive but weak predictor (rho 0.20-0.59, all positive under item-clustered resampling) whose usefulness is regime-dependent: best for unsaturated mid-tier models and for allocating compute, and worst -- over-confident yet no more accurate -- for the most consistent frontier model (agreement >=0.8 on 77% of GPQA case-result entries, 48% of those wrong). An exploratory cross-family check on three Claude tiers shows the same frontier over-confidence, with confident errors recurring across providers above a marginal-preserving null. Self-consistency is thus a conditional proxy for correctness, not a standalone confidence score. We publicly release the de-identified per-run rows and answer distributions.

</details>


### [80] [Persuasion Attacks Can Decrease Effectiveness of CoT Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08066)
*Jennifer Za,Julija Bainiaksina,Nikita Ostrovsky,Tanush Chopra,Victoria Krakovna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现链式思维监控在面对对抗性说服时反而使有害行为批准率增加9.5%，并提出了跨模型家族的事实核查监控框架，将违规批准率降低45%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLM容易受到基于说服的越狱攻击，但尚不清楚这种漏洞是否扩展到监控LLM的场景——即对抗性智能体能否说服其链式思维监控器批准违反政策的提案。

Method: 设计了包含40个任务的评估框架，分析数千次智能体-监控器交互；提出事实核查监控框架，使用不同模型家族的监控器与事实核查器配对。

Result: 对抗性场景下，监控器访问CoT推理使有害行为批准率平均增加9.5%；跨模型家族的事实核查配对（如Claude 3.7 Sonnet监控器+GPT-4.1事实核查器）将违规批准率降低45%，而同模型配对仅降低6%。

Conclusion: CoT监控本身可能不足以抵御对抗性说服，模型多样化的联合事实核查提供了鲁棒的缓解方案。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a promising safety mechanism for AI agents, based on the premise that visible reasoning traces can surface misaligned or deceptive behavior. While effective in standard scenarios, recent work highlights that LLMs remain vulnerable to persuasion-based jailbreaks, where natural-language arguments override model constraints. We stress-test whether this vulnerability extends to monitoring LLMs: can an adversarial agent persuade its CoT monitor to approve proposed actions that violate the monitor's policy? We design an evaluation framework with 40 tasks and analyze thousands of agent-monitor interactions, where agents are instructed to argue for policy-violating proposals. We find that in such adversarial settings, monitor access to the agent's CoT reasoning increases rather than decreases approval of harmful actions on average by 9.5%, as the scratchpad provides an additional persuasion channel. To address this, we introduce a fact-checking monitoring framework. We find that a fact-checker and monitor pairing from different model families, for example a Claude 3.7 Sonnet monitor paired with a GPT-4.1 fact-checker, reduces approval of policy-violating actions by up to 45%, compared to only 6%, when using the same model for both fact-checking and monitoring roles. Our results demonstrate that CoT monitoring alone may be insufficient against adversarial persuasion, and that model-diverse fact-checking provides a robust mitigation.

</details>


### [81] [PARA-PV: Physics-Aware Retrieval-Augmented PV Prediction Based on Frozen Foundation Model and Distribution Shift Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08079)
*Hang Fan,Weican Liu,Ying Lu,Dunnan Liu,Long Cheng,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PARA-PV框架，通过物理感知检索增强、时序基础模型校准、分布偏移校正和物理约束损失函数，实现高精度光伏功率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏发电受天气变化、昼夜交替、状态依赖动态和物理约束共同影响，准确预测对电网调度和可再生能源并网至关重要，但现有方法难以充分嵌入物理知识。

Method: 1) 物理感知检索增强学习：编码多变量PV观测为patch表示，检索历史中形状、功率水平、运行状态和时段一致的轨迹；2) Chronos时序基础模型残差校准；3) 物理感知分布偏移校正：基于功率、天气、时间、昼夜条件进行门控均值和尺度修正；4) 物理约束损失函数：将样本划分为峰值、爬坡、夜间和常规四种工况，自适应加权。

Result: 代码已开源，实验结果表明该方法能有效提升PV功率预测精度，特别是在峰值、爬坡和夜间等运行关键状态上。

Conclusion: PARA-PV通过全流程嵌入物理知识，解决了物理一致性与泛化能力的平衡问题，为光伏功率预测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Accurate photovoltaic (PV) power forecasting is essential for reliable grid dispatch and renewable energy integration, yet it remains challenging because PV generation is jointly shaped by weather variability, day-night transitions, regime-dependent dynamics, and strict physical constraints. We propose PARA-PV, a Physics-Aware Retrieval-Augmented framework that embeds physical knowledge throughout the forecasting process. The framework first encodes multivariate PV observations into patch-level representations and, through a physics-aware retrieval-augmented learner, retrieves historical patches and analog trajectories that are consistent with the current window in temporal shape, power level, PV operating state, and intra-day period; this yields a physically grounded base forecast. To supplement local memory with broader temporal knowledge, the base forecast is then calibrated against a frozen Chronos time-series foundation-model prior through a lightweight residual adapter, so that general temporal regularities are adapted to PV-specific dynamics without overriding the physically grounded prediction. Because residual conditional distribution shifts persist when weather and diurnal regimes change, a physics-aware distribution shift correction module subsequently adjusts the preliminary forecast using power, weather, timestamp, and day/night conditions, applying gated mean-shift and scale corrections selectively. Finally, a physics-constrained loss function partitions the samples into peak, ramping, night-time, and regular regimes and adaptively reweights their error contributions, preventing the dominant regular regime from suppressing learning of operationally critical states. Our code is available at https://github.com/weican1103/PARA-PV.

</details>


### [82] [CausalDS: Benchmarking Causal Reasoning in Data-Science Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08093)
*Andrej Leban,Yuekai Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CausalDS是一个用于评估大语言模型在数据科学工作流中进行因果推理能力的基准，它结合了结构化因果模型、合成数据生成、自然语言故事以及覆盖Pearl三阶梯的多样化任务，同时包含编码、工具使用和弃权评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准要么侧重符号因果推理但缺乏真实数据分析场景，要么侧重数据分析但缺乏原理性因果结构；同时现有因果评估数据集多依赖有限模板化变体而非系统性生成新因果结构，因此需要一个更全面、可扩展的因果推理评估基准。

Method: 每个实例包含一个采样的结构因果模型（SCM）、生成的观测数据以及基于真实领域的合成自然语言故事；任务覆盖Pearl三阶梯（包括数据科学预测任务作为Rung 1），多数任务需要编码和工具使用，并引入观测模型模拟不完美观测；将“弃权”作为一等评分结果。

Result: CausalDS系统性地生成了具有原则性因果结构的多样化场景，覆盖了从符号因果推理到数据科学编码、工具使用、不确定性量化及弃权决策的全面评估维度。

Conclusion: CausalDS提供了首个将因果推理、数据科学工作流、工具使用和弃权评估整合为一体的基准，有助于全面评估LLM在代理式数据科学中的因果推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly act as integrated data-science agents, combining abstract reasoning with advanced tool use. Yet the relevant benchmark landscape largely divides into symbolic causal reasoning benchmarks without realistic data analysis or data analysis benchmarks without a principled causal data-generating structure. Furthermore, existing causal evaluation datasets are often restricted to curated examples from existing sources, with diversity coming from limited templatized variations rather than from systematic generation of novel synthetic causal structures. We introduce CausalDS, a benchmark for evaluating causal reasoning in agentic data-science workflows. Each benchmark instance is a scene consisting of a sampled structural causal model (SCM) with generated observational data and an accompanying synthetic natural-language story grounded in a realistic domain. We optionally ground the composition of the benchmark components in empirical distributions obtained from real-world datasets, thus retaining empirical structure while reducing the "causal parrot" risk through completely synthetic generation. From each scene, we then derive tasks spanning all three of Pearl's rungs, with typical data-science prediction tasks appearing as Rung 1. Most tasks include a data science coding component, where the model typically needs to use several tools to arrive at the final answer due to the frequent presence of imperfect observations, which are generated by an observation model. Additionally, recognizing when a question admits no warranted answer and abstaining is treated as a first-class scored outcome. The benchmark thus jointly evaluates symbolic causal reasoning, data science, uncertainty quantification, abstention, and tool use/coding.

</details>


### [83] [Answer Set Programming Energised! End-to-End Neurosymbolic Reasoning and Learning with ASP and Energy Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08136)
*Jakob Suchan,Julius Monsen,Salim Baloch,Mehul Bhatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回答集编程（ASP）与能量基模型模块化整合的神经符号推理与学习方法，支持连续潜在空间中的联合优化，具备完整的ASP声明式语义，并能进行端到端鲁棒训练，在视觉问答（Clevr）和多目标跟踪（MOT）基准上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法在整合背景知识、约束和非单调推理方面存在不足，且缺乏支持连续潜在空间联合优化及端到端训练的通用框架。

Method: 将回答集编程（ASP）与能量基模型（EBM）模块化整合，通过ASP声明式语义（融入背景知识、约束、非单调推理）在连续潜在空间中实现联合优化，并基于回答集模理论（ASMT）提供通用模型与平台。

Result: 在MNIST上验证了基本用法，在Clevr视觉问答和MOT多目标跟踪基准上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该方法为ASP中心化的鲁棒端到端训练提供了通用框架，适用于涉及感知与交互的动态领域应用。

Abstract: We present a general neurosymbolic reasoning and learning methodology based on a modular integration of answer set programming with an energy based model substrate. Key contributions are: (1) supporting joint optimisation in the continuous latent space through explicit ASP-based declarative semantics fully incorporating background knowledge, constraints, non-monotonic inference; and (2) advancing recent works at the interface of answer sets, probabilistic logic, and answer set modulo theories by providing a generalised model and practical platform for ASP-centric robust, end-to-end training for applications in dynamic domains (e.g., involving perception and interaction). We provide a practical implementation, and demonstrate basic use and application (with MNIST), and evaluate with the visual question-answering benchmark Clevr and the multi-object tracking benchmark MOT.

</details>


### [84] [Overthinking: Amplifying Reasoning Weights to Extract Learned Secrets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08173)
*Jack Hopkins,Dipika Khullar,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"过度思考"（overthinking）方法，通过推理任务向量放大推理模型的"边想边说"倾向，从而在审计过程中更有效地揭示隐藏信息。该方法通过参数插值放大推理能力，并引入层级衰减策略保持输出质量。实验表明，过度思考模型比原始推理模型暴露隐藏信息的频率高出最多10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的黑盒审计可能遗漏微妙的失调和隐藏信息，需要更有效的方法在审计过程中揭示这些隐藏信息。

Method: 定义过度思考模型为 θ_Oα = θ_M + α(θ_R - θ_M)，其中α>1放大推理能力；引入层级衰减策略选择性放大推理同时保持输出质量和连贯性。

Result: 在四种实验设置下、2B-32B模型上，过度思考模型比原始推理模型暴露隐藏信息的频率高出最多10倍；不同秘密类型对推理方向扰动和权重扰动的敏感性不同。

Conclusion: 推理放大可以更频繁地揭示训练过程中获得的秘密或意外行为，为语言模型审计提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: Black box auditing of language models is an essential pre-deployment tool, but it may miss subtle forms of misalignment and hidden information. To better elicit hidden information during an auditing process, we introduce \emph{overthinking}: the process of using reasoning task vectors to amplify the propensity to think out loud of reasoning models. Given the parameters of a non-reasoning instruct model $M$ and reasoning-distilled model $R$, we define the \emph{overthinking model} as $\boldsymbolθ_{\mathcal{O}_α} = \boldsymbolθ_{\mathcal{M}} + α(\boldsymbolθ_{\mathcal{R}} - \boldsymbolθ_{\mathcal{M}})$, where $α> 1$ amplifies reasoning beyond the pure reasoning model $R$. Additionally, we introduce new layer-wise attenuation strategies that selectively amplify reasoning without losing quality and coherence of model outputs.
